      "parsedText": "Overview \nWe use this onboarding process for all full-time, part-time, and long-term contractors who \nwill be working in a desk-based environment (remote, hybrid, or in-office). \nOnboarding has three parts: \n1. Pre-start setup (before Day 1) \n2. First-day experience \n3. First two weeks \nPre-Start Setup (at least 3 days before start date) \nOffer & Documentation \n● HR sends offer letter, NDAs, and tax paperwork (W-4, I-9, direct deposit). \n● New hire signs all forms electronically. Keep copies in /People/New Hires/YYYY folder. \n● Confirm legal name, preferred name, and personal email for setup. \nSystems & Equipment \n● CTO creates all required accounts: \n○ Email (Google Workspace) \n● Ship or prepare laptop, monitor, and any accessories. \n○ Equipment is logged in the Asset Tracker sheet with serial numbers and \nassigned user. \n○ Send a quick “Welcome Kit” email with setup instructions and Day 1 agenda. \n \nInternal Prep \n● Add start date to team calendar. \n● Announce via email: “🎉 New hire alert! [Name] joins [Team] as [Role]. Starts [date].” \n● Assign a buddy / point of contact for the first week. \n● Manager prepares a 30-day ramp plan (goals, key projects, training sessions) & shares \nwith new hire. \n \nFirst-Day Experience \nAccess Check \n● Confirm the following work properly: \n○ Email + calendar \n● If something doesn’t work, flag to CTO immediately. \nPaperwork Confirmation \n● Verify completion of W-4, I-9, Direct Deposit form, NDA. \n● Upload copies to the secure HR folder. \n \nManager 1:1 \n● Discuss role expectations, first-week goals, communication preferences. \n● Review the 30-day onboarding plan. \n● Schedule recurring check-ins. \nTeam Intro \n● Send team intro email  \n● Set up coffee chats \nTips & Notes \n● Always over-communicate the first week. No one remembers everything on Day 1. \n● Keep the onboarding checklist lightweight and digital — ideally in Notion or ClickUp. \n● Pair new hires with someone outside their direct team for cross-org visibility. \n● Celebrate small wins — it sets the tone for the rest of their time here."
